\displaystyle\leq F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}}{2}+\bar{\rho}\eta\Bigg\{\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg[-\frac{\bar{\rho}}{2}\|(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})-(\hat{\mathbf{w}}_{t},\hat{\mathbf{s}}_{t},\hat{s}^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{1}}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\big[\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|+\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|+\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|^{2}+\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle+C_{1}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})-u_{i,t}\bigg\|\bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(b)}}{{\leq}}F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}}{n_{+}n_{-}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\big[\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|+\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|+\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|^{2}+\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|^{2}\big]

\displaystyle+\frac{\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})-u_{i,t}\bigg\|

where in inequality (a) we use
\bar{\rho}=\rho_{F}+\rho_{g}C_{f}+8\rho_{g}C_{f}C_{h}+C_{f}C_{g}L_{h}
and
C_{1}=\max\{2C_{f}C_{g},2\rho_{g}C_{f},2C_{f}\}
, and inequality (b) uses Lemma
3.2
.

With general error bounds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\mathbb{E}[\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|]\leq(1-\mu_{1})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|+R_{1},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\mathbb{E}[\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|]\leq(1-\mu_{2})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|+R_{2},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\mathbb{E}[\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{i,t}\|^{2}]\leq(1-\mu_{1})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|^{2}+R_{3},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\mathbb{E}[\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-v_{j,t}\|^{2}]\leq(1-\mu_{2})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|^{2}+R_{4},

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})-u_{i,t}\bigg\|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\mu_{3})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})-u_{i,0}\bigg\|+R_{5},

we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1},\mathbf{s}_{t+1},s^{\prime}_{t+1})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})]+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}\bigg[(1-\mu_{1})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|+(1-\mu_{2})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|

\displaystyle+(1-\mu_{1})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|^{2}+(1-\mu_{2})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1-\mu_{3})^{t}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})-u_{i,0}\bigg\|+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})]+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}\bigg[(1-\mu_{min})^{t}\bigg(\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})-u_{i,0}\bigg\|\bigg)+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5}\bigg]

where
\mu_{min}=\min\{\mu_{1},\mu_{2},\mu_{3}\}
.

Taking summation from
t=0
to
T-1
yields

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{T},\mathbf{s}_{T},s^{\prime}_{T})]

\displaystyle\leq F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{0},\mathbf{s}_{0},s^{\prime}_{0})+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}T}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}\bigg[\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(1-\mu_{min})^{t}\bigg(\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})-u_{i,0}\bigg\|\bigg)+T(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5})\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{0},\mathbf{s}_{0},s^{\prime}_{0})+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}T}{2}-\frac{\eta}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}\bigg[\frac{\Delta_{0}}{\mu_{min}}+T(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5})\bigg]

where we use
\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}(1-\mu_{min})^{t}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{min}}
and define constant
\Delta_{0}
such that

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\|h_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{i,0}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}\|h_{j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})-v_{j,0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{X_{i}\in S_{+}}\bigg\|\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{X_{j}\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})-u_{i,0}\bigg\|\bigg)\leq\Delta_{0}.

Then it follows

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta T}\Bigg[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{0},\mathbf{s}_{0},s^{\prime}_{0})-\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{T},\mathbf{s}_{T},s^{\prime}_{T})]+\frac{3\eta^{2}\bar{\rho}M^{2}T}{2}

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\eta C_{1}\bigg[\frac{\Delta_{0}}{\mu_{min}}+T(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5})\bigg]\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\Delta}{\eta T}+(2+\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}})\eta\bar{\rho}M^{2}+\frac{2\bar{\rho}C_{1}\Delta_{0}}{\mu_{min}T}+2\bar{\rho}C_{1}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{T}(\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{1}{\mu_{min}})+\eta+R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}+R_{4}+R_{5}\right)

where we define constant
\Delta
such that
F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{0},\mathbf{s}_{0},s^{\prime}_{0})-\mathbb{E}[F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{T},\mathbf{s}_{T},s^{\prime}_{T})]\leq\Delta
.

With MSVR updates for
v_{i,t}
and
u_{i,t}
, following from Lemma
C.5
and Lemma
C.6
, we have

\displaystyle\mu_{1}=\frac{B_{1}\tau_{1}}{2n_{+}},\quad\mu_{2}=\frac{B_{1}\tau_{1}}{2n_{-}},\quad\mu_{3}=\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}}

\displaystyle R_{1}=\frac{2\tau_{1}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{+}C_{h}M\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{1}^{1/2}},\quad R_{2}=\frac{2\tau_{1}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{-}C_{h}M\eta}{B_{2}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle R_{3}=\frac{4\tau_{1}\sigma^{2}}{B_{3}}+\frac{16n_{+}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau_{1}},\quad R_{4}=\frac{4\tau_{1}\sigma^{2}}{B_{3}}+\frac{16n_{-}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{2}^{2}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle R_{5}=\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}}+\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}})\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}})\frac{\eta}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\frac{1}{T}(\frac{1}{\eta}+\frac{1}{\mu_{min}})+(\frac{\tau_{1}^{1/2}}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}})\sigma

\displaystyle+\frac{n_{+}\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{-}\eta}{B_{2}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}\max\{\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}},\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}}\}\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}\max\{\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}},\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}}\}\frac{\eta}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}\Bigg)

Setting

\displaystyle\tau_{1}=\mathcal{O}\left(\min\left\{B_{3},\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}\min\{\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}},\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}}\}B_{2}^{1/2}\right\}\epsilon^{4}\right),\quad\tau_{2}=\mathcal{O}(B_{2}\epsilon^{4}),

\displaystyle\eta=\mathcal{O}\left(\min\left\{\min\{\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}},\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}}\}\min\{B_{3}^{1/2},\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}}\min\{\frac{n_{+}^{1/4}}{B_{1}^{1/4}},\frac{n_{-}^{1/4}}{B_{2}^{1/4}}\}B_{2}^{1/4}\},\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}\min\{\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}},\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}}\}B_{3}^{1/2}\right\}\epsilon^{4}\right),

Then with

T\geq\mathcal{O}\left(\max\left\{\max\{\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}},\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}}\}\max\{\frac{1}{B_{3}^{1/2}},\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}}\max\{\frac{B_{1}^{1/4}}{n_{+}^{1/4}},\frac{B_{2}^{1/4}}{n_{-}^{1/4}}\}\frac{1}{B_{2}^{1/4}}\},\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}\max\{\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}},\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}}\}\frac{1}{B_{2}^{1/2}}\right\}\epsilon^{-6}\right)

iterations, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\nabla F_{1/\bar{\rho}}(\mathbf{w}_{t},\mathbf{s}_{t},s^{\prime}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}.

∎

Appendix D
Proofs of Lemmas and Propositions

D.1
Additional Proposition

Proposition D.1
.

Consider a Lipschitz continuous function
f:O\to\mathbb{R}
where
O\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
is an open set. Assume
f
to be non-increasing (resp. non-decreasing) with respect to each element in the input, then all subgradients of
f
are element-wise non-positive (resp. non-negative).

Proof of Proposition
D.1
.

Let
D
be the subset of
O
where
f
is differentiable. By Theorem 9.60 in
[
24
]
, a Lipschitz continuous function
f:O\to\mathbb{R}
, where
O\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
is an open set, is differentiable almost everywhere, i.e.,
D
is dense in
O
. Then by Theorem 9.61 in
[
24
]
, the subdifferential of
f
at
x
is defined as

\partial f(x)=\text{con}\{v|\exists x_{k}\to x\text{ with }x_{k}\in D,\nabla f(x_{k})\to v\},

where con denotes the convex hull. If we assume that
f
is non-increasing with respect to each element in the input, then
\nabla f(x)\leq 0
(element-wise) for all differentiable points
x\in D
. It implies that the all vectors in
\{v|\exists x_{k}\to x\text{ with }x_{k}\in D,\nabla f(x_{k})\to v\}
are element-wise non-positive. Therefore, all subgradients of
f
are element-wise non-positive. On the other hand, if we assume that
f
is non-decreasing, one may follow the same argument and conclude that all subgradients of
f
are element-wise non-negative.
∎

For functions
f:O\to\mathbb{R}^{m}
where
O\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
is an open set, one may write
f=(f_{1},\dots,f_{m})
and apply the above proposition for each
f_{k},k=1,\dots,m
.

D.2
Proofs of Proposition
4.2
and Proposition
4.4

To prove Proposition
4.2
and Proposition
4.4
, we first present the following proposition on the weak-convexity of composition functions.

Proposition D.2
.

Assume
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
is
\rho_{1}
-weakly-convex and
C_{1}
-Lipschitz continuous,
g:\mathbb{R}^{\bar{d}}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
is
C_{2}
-Lipschitz continuous, and either of the followings holds:

1.

f(\cdot)
is monotone and
g(\cdot)
is
L_{2}
-smooth;

2.

f(\cdot)
is non-decreasing and
g(\cdot)
is
L_{2}
-weakly-convex,

then
f\circ g
is
\tilde{\rho}
-weakly-convex with
\tilde{\rho}=\sqrt{d}L_{2}C_{1}+\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}
.

Proof of Proposition
D.2
.

The weak convexity of
f
implies

\displaystyle f(g(y))

\displaystyle\geq f(g(x))+v^{\top}(g(y)-g(x))-\frac{\rho_{1}}{2}\|g(y)-g(x)\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq f(g(x))+v^{\top}(g(y)-g(x))-\frac{\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}

where
v\in\partial f(g(x))
. Moreover, due to the smoothness of
g(\cdot)
(or weakly-convexity of
g(\cdot)
, then only the second inequality holds), we have

\displaystyle g(y)-g(x)\leq\nabla g(x)^{\top}(y-x)+\mathbf{v}\left(\frac{L_{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}\right),

(44)

\displaystyle g(y)-g(x)\geq\nabla g(x)^{\top}(y-x)-\mathbf{v}\left(\frac{L_{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}\right).

where
\mathbf{v}(e)
denotes a
d
-dimensional vector with value
e
on each dimensions. We first assume that
f
is non-increasing, then we may use the first inequality in (
44
) and the Lipschitz continuity of
g
to get

\displaystyle f(g(y))

\displaystyle\geq f(g(x))+v^{\top}\left[\nabla g(x)^{\top}(y-x)+\mathbf{v}\left(\frac{L_{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}\right)\right]-\frac{\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq f(g(x))+v^{\top}\nabla g(x)^{\top}(y-x)+v^{\top}\mathbf{v}\left(\frac{L_{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}\right)-\frac{\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq f(g(x))+\langle v^{\top}\nabla g(x)^{\top}(y-x)-\frac{\sqrt{d}L_{2}C_{1}+\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}}{2}\|x-y\|^{2}.

On the other hand, if we assume
f
is non-decreasing, the same result follows from the second inequality in (
44
). Thus
f\circ g
is
\tilde{\rho}
-weakly-convex with
\tilde{\rho}_{g}=\sqrt{d}L_{2}C_{1}+\rho_{1}C_{2}^{2}
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
4.2
.

Under Assumption
4.1
, Proposition
D.2
directly implies the
\rho_{F}
-weak-convexity of
F(\mathbf{w})
with
\rho_{F}=\sqrt{d_{1}}\rho_{g}C_{f}+\rho_{f}C_{g}^{2}
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
4.4
.

Under Assumption
4.3
, we first apply Proposition
D.2
to the composite function
g_{i}(h_{i,j}(\cdot))
and obtain its
\rho_{\tilde{g}}=\sqrt{d_{2}}L_{h}C_{g}+\rho_{g}C_{h}^{2}
-weak-convexity. To show it Lipschitz continuity, we use the Lipschitz continuity of
g_{i}
and
h_{i,j}
to obtain

\displaystyle\|g_{i}(h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}))-g_{i}(h_{i,j}(\tilde{\mathbf{w}}))\|^{2}\leq C_{g}^{2}C_{h}^{2}\|\mathbf{w}-\tilde{\mathbf{w}}\|^{2}.

Thus
g_{i}(h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}))
is
C_{\tilde{g}}=C_{g}C_{h}
-Lipschitz-continuous

Since we assume
f_{i}(\cdot)
is non-decreasing,
\rho_{f}
-weakly-convex and
C_{f}
-Lipschitz continuous, and
g_{i}(h_{i,j}(\cdot))
is
\rho_{\tilde{g}}
-weakly-convex and
C_{\tilde{g}}
-Lipschitz-continuous, we apply Proposition
D.2
again to conclude that
F(\cdot)
is
\rho_{F}=\sqrt{d_{1}}\rho_{\tilde{g}}C_{f}+\rho_{f}C_{\tilde{g}}^{2}
-weakly-convex.
∎

D.3
Proof of Lemma
4.5

Proof of Lemma
4.5
.

With
\gamma=\frac{n_{1}-B_{1}}{B_{1}(1-\tau)}+(1-\tau)
,
\tau\leq\frac{1}{2}
, MSVR update gives recursive error bound
[
15
]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{n_{1}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{n_{1}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\eta^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|G_{t}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}

Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\bigg(\frac{2\tau^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{+}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{16n_{1}^{2}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{2n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau}
.

It follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{16n_{1}^{2}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{1}C_{g}M\eta}{B_{1}\tau^{1/2}}\bigg]^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{1}C_{g}M\eta}{B_{1}\tau^{1/2}}

Taking summation over
i\in\mathcal{S}
, we obtain the desired result

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}}\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{t+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{4nC_{g}M\eta}{B_{1}\tau^{1/2}}

∎

D.4
Proof of Lemma
C.6

Proof of Lemma
C.6
.

With
\gamma_{3}=\frac{n_{+}-B_{1}}{B_{1}(1-\tau_{2})}+(1-\tau_{2})
and
\tau_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, MSVR update gives the following recursive error bound
[
15
]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|^{2}]

(45)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{+}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{+}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{8n_{+}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\mathbb{E}[\|(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})-(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{+}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{+}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{16n_{+}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,t}-v_{i,t+1}\|^{2}+\|v_{j,t}-v_{j,t+1}\|^{2}]+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}

It remains to bound
\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,t}-v_{i,t+1}\|^{2}]
and
\mathbb{E}[\|v_{j,t}-v_{j,t+1}\|^{2}]
. We bound the former, and the latter’s bound naturally follows. Consider the update of
v_{i,t+1}
and we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,t}-v_{i,t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}\|\tau_{1}v_{i,t}-\tau_{1}h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})-\gamma_{1}(h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})-h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t-1};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t}))\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2B_{1}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{+}}\|v_{i,t}-h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}\gamma_{1}^{2}}{n_{+}}\|h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})-h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t-1};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2B_{1}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{+}}\|v_{i,t}-h^{(i)}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i}^{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}\gamma_{1}^{2}C_{h}}{n_{+}}\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\frac{8B_{1}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}}{n_{+}}+\frac{8n_{+}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}}{B_{1}}

where inequality (a) uses
\tau_{1}\leq 1/2
and
\gamma_{1}=\frac{n_{+}-B_{1}}{B_{1}(1-\tau_{1})}+(1-\tau_{1})\leq\frac{2n_{+}}{B_{1}}
. Plugging the above inequality back into inequality (
45
) gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{+}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{+}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{16n_{+}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\left(8\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})+8C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\right)+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{+}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t}-v_{i,t},s_{i,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{+}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+128C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})\tau_{1}^{2}+128C_{g}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\eta^{2}+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}

Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t-j)}\bigg(\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{+}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+128C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})\tau_{1}^{2}+128C_{g}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\eta^{2}+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}

\displaystyle+256C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})\frac{\tau_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}+256C_{g}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\frac{\eta^{2}}{\tau_{2}}+\frac{16n_{+}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})\frac{\tau_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\frac{\eta^{2}}{\tau_{2}}+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau_{2}}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{2n_{+}}{B_{1}\tau_{1}}
and denotes
C_{2}^{2}=2\max\{256C_{g}^{2}M^{2},256C_{g}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2},16C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\}
. Taking average over
i\in S_{+}
gives the squared-norm error bound.

To derive the norm error bound, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{B_{1}}{n_{+}}+\frac{B_{2}}{n_{-}})\frac{\tau_{1}^{2}}{\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}}(\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}+\frac{n_{-}}{B_{2}})\frac{\eta^{2}}{\tau_{2}}+C_{2}^{2}\frac{n_{+}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}}+\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}})\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}})\frac{\eta}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}\bigg]^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}}+\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}})\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}})\frac{\eta}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

Taking average over
i\in S_{+}
, we obtain the norm error bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{i\in S_{+}}\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{j,t+1}-v_{i,t+1},s_{i,t+1})\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{+}})^{t+1}\frac{1}{n_{+}}\sum_{i\in S_{+}}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{-}}\sum_{j\in S_{-}}g_{i}(v_{i,0}-v_{j,0},s_{i,0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{B_{1}^{1/2}}{n_{+}^{1/2}}+\frac{B_{2}^{1/2}}{n_{-}^{1/2}})\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}}{B_{1}}(\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}}{B_{1}^{1/2}}+\frac{n_{-}^{1/2}}{B_{2}^{1/2}})\frac{\eta}{\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+C_{2}\frac{n_{+}^{1/2}\eta}{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

∎

D.5
Proof of Lemma
A.3

Proof of Lemma
A.3
.

With
\gamma_{1}=\frac{n_{1}n_{2}-B_{1}B_{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}(1-\tau_{1})}+(1-\tau_{1})
and
\tau_{1}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, MSVR update has the following recursive error bound
[
15
]
[
15
]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{n_{1}n_{2}})\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{1}^{2}B_{1}B_{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}B_{3}}+\frac{8n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{1}^{2}B_{1}B_{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}B_{3}}+\frac{8n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}}

Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2(t+1)}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2(t-j)}(\frac{2\tau_{1}^{2}B_{1}B_{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}B_{3}}+\frac{8n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}})

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2(t+1)}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{1}\sigma^{2}}{B_{3}}+\frac{16n_{1}^{2}n_{2}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}B_{2}^{2}\tau_{1}}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{2n_{1}n_{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}
. Taking average over
(i,j)\in S_{1}\times S_{2}
gives the squared-norm error bound.

To derive the norm error bound, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{2(t+1)}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{1}\sigma^{2}}{B_{3}}+\frac{16n_{1}^{2}n_{2}^{2}C_{h}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}B_{2}^{2}\tau_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{t+1}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{1}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}M\eta}{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}\bigg]^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{t+1}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{1}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}M\eta}{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}

Taking average over
(i,j)\in\mathcal{S}_{1}\times\mathcal{S}_{2}
, we obtain the norm error bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{n_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}_{1}}\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}\|v_{i,j,t+1}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}}{2n_{1}n_{2}})^{t+1}\frac{1}{n_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}_{1}}\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}\|v_{i,j,0}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{1}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{3}^{1/2}}+\frac{4n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}M\eta}{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{1/2}}.

∎

D.6
Proof of Lemma
A.4

Proof of Lemma
A.4
.

With
\gamma_{2}=\frac{n_{1}-B_{1}}{B_{1}(1-\tau_{2})}+(1-\tau_{2})
and
\tau_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, MSVR update has the following recursive error bound
[
15
]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

(46)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{1}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-v_{i,j,t}\|^{2}]

It remains to bound
\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-v_{i,j,t}\|^{2}]
, which is done as following

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|v_{i,j,t+1}-v_{i,j,t}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{B_{1}B_{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\|\tau_{1}v_{i,j,t}-\tau_{1}h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})-\gamma_{1}(h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t-1};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t}))\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\|v_{i,j,t}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}B_{2}\gamma_{1}^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\|h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t-1};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\|v_{i,j,t}-h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}B_{2}\gamma_{1}^{2}C_{h}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(a)}}{{\leq}}\frac{8B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}}

where inequality (a) uses
\tau_{1}\leq 1/2
and
\gamma_{1}=\frac{n_{1}n_{2}-B_{1}B_{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}(1-\tau_{1})}+(1-\tau_{1})\leq\frac{2n_{1}n_{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}}
. Plugging the above inequality back into inequality (
46
) gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{1}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}}\left(\frac{8B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}}{B_{1}B_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{n_{1}})\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{64B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{n_{2}}+\frac{64n_{1}^{2}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}B_{2}}

Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{t-j}\bigg(\frac{2\tau_{2}^{2}B_{1}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{64B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{n_{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{64n_{1}^{2}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}B_{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{128n_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}n_{2}\tau_{2}}+\frac{128n_{1}^{3}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}^{3}B_{2}\tau_{2}}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{2n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau_{1}}
. Taking average over
i\in\mathcal{S}_{1}
gives the squared-norm error bound.

To derive the norm error bound, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\frac{4\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{128n_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}n_{2}\tau_{2}}+\frac{128n_{1}^{3}n_{2}C_{h}^{2}\eta^{2}M^{2}C_{g}^{2}}{B_{1}^{3}B_{2}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{8\sqrt{2}n_{1}^{1/2}B_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{1}MC_{g}}{B_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{8\sqrt{2}n_{1}^{3/2}n_{2}^{1/2}C_{h}\eta MC_{g}}{B_{1}^{3/2}B_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}\bigg]^{2}

Taking squared root on both sides and taking average over
i\in S_{1}
, we obtain the norm error bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{n_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}_{1}}\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{t+1}\frac{1}{n_{1}}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{S}_{1}}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|+\frac{2\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{C_{2}n_{1}^{1/2}B_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{1}}{B_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{C_{2}n_{1}^{3/2}n_{2}^{1/2}\eta}{B_{1}^{3/2}B_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{2}^{1/2}}

where
C_{2}=\max\{8\sqrt{2}MC_{g},8\sqrt{2}C_{h}MC_{g}\}
.
∎

D.7
Proof of Lemma
B.2

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

Define

\tilde{u}_{i,t}=(1-\tau)u_{i,t}+\tau g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t})

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}[\|\tilde{u}_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}[\|(1-\tau)(u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t}))+\tau(g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t})-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t}))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}[(1-\tau)^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\tau^{2}\|g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t})-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\tau)\tau\langle u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t}),g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t})-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\tau)^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}

It follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{1}^{t}}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}[\|\tilde{u}_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}(1-\tau)^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}

where we use

\displaystyle\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}(1-\tau)^{2}+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})

\displaystyle=\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}(1-2\tau+\tau^{2})+1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}

\displaystyle=1-2\tau\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}+\tau^{2}\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\tau\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq 1-\tau\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}+(\frac{\tau B_{1}}{2n_{1}})^{2}=(1-\frac{\tau B_{1}}{2n_{1}})^{2}

Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau})\|g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{2n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})\frac{B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau})C_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau}C_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}\|\mathbf{w}_{t}-\mathbf{w}_{t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}\tau}

where we use
\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}}\leq 1
. Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}\tau}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\bigg[\frac{2B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8n_{1}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}\tau}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{8\tau\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{32n_{1}^{2}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau}
.

To obtain the absolute bound, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|^{2}+\frac{8\tau\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{32n_{1}^{2}C_{g}^{2}M^{2}\eta^{2}}{B_{1}^{2}\tau^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left[(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau}{4n_{1}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-g_{i}(\mathbf{w}_{0})\|+\frac{2\sqrt{2}\tau^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}n_{1}C_{g}M\eta}{B_{1}\tau}\right]^{2}

The desired result follows by taking squared root on both sides.
∎

D.8
Proof of Lemma
B.5

Proof of Lemma
B.5
.

The proof of Lemma
B.5
is the same as Lemma
B.2
.
∎

D.9
Proof of Lemma
B.6

Proof of Lemma
B.6
.

Define

\tilde{u}_{i,t}=(1-\tau_{2})u_{i,t}+\tau_{2}\frac{1}{B_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}[\|\tilde{u}_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}[\|(1-\tau_{2})(u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t}))+\tau_{2}(\frac{1}{B_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t}))\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}[(1-\tau_{2})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+\tau_{2}^{2}\|\frac{1}{B_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2(1-\tau_{2})\tau_{2}\langle u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t}),\frac{1}{B_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\rangle]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\tau_{2})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+\frac{\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}

It follows

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2,i}^{t}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{1}^{t}}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{2}^{t}}[\|\tilde{u}_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}(1-\tau_{2})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+\frac{B_{1}\tau^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}

where we use

\displaystyle\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}}(1-\tau_{2})^{2}+(1-\frac{B_{1}}{n_{1}})\leq(1-\frac{\tau_{2}B_{1}}{2n_{1}})^{2}

Then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{t}\left[(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau_{2}})\|\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{2n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+(1+\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}

\displaystyle+(1+\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau_{2}})C_{g}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{t}[\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}\|v_{i,j,t}-v_{i,j,t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2}\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}+\frac{2B_{1}\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{2}\tau_{2}}

where we use
\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}}\leq 1
, and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{t}[\|v_{i,j,t}-v_{i,j,t+1}\|^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{B_{1}B_{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}\mathbb{E}_{\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t}}\|\tau_{1}v_{i,j,t}-\tau_{1}h_{i,j}(\mathbf{w}_{t};\mathcal{B}_{3,i,j}^{t})\|^{2}\leq\frac{B_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}M^{2}}{n_{1}n_{2}}.

Applying this inequality recursively, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t})\|^{2}]+\frac{2B_{1}\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{2}\tau_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\bigg[\frac{2B_{1}\tau_{2}^{2}\sigma^{2}}{n_{1}B_{2}}+\frac{8C_{g}^{2}M^{2}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{n_{2}\tau_{2}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\frac{8\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{32C_{g}^{2}M^{2}n_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{B_{1}n_{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}

where we use
\sum_{j=0}^{t}(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2(t-j)}\leq\frac{4n_{1}}{B_{1}\tau_{2}}
.

To obtain the absolute bound, we derive

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\|u_{i,t+1}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,t+1})\|^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{2(t+1)}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|^{2}+\frac{8\tau_{2}\sigma^{2}}{B_{2}}+\frac{32C_{g}^{2}M^{2}n_{1}B_{2}\tau_{1}^{2}}{B_{1}n_{2}\tau_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left[(1-\frac{B_{1}\tau_{2}}{4n_{1}})^{t+1}\|u_{i,0}-\frac{1}{n_{2}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{2}}g_{i}(v_{i,j,0})\|+\frac{2\sqrt{2}\tau_{2}^{1/2}\sigma}{B_{2}^{1/2}}+\frac{4\sqrt{2}C_{g}Mn_{1}^{1/2}B_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{1}}{B_{1}^{1/2}n_{2}^{1/2}\tau_{2}}\right]^{2}

The desired result follows by taking squared root on both sides.
∎

Appendix E
Group Distributionally Robust Optimization

NSWC FCCO finds an important application in group distributionally robust optimization (group DRO), particularly valuable in addressing distributional shift
[
25
]
. Consider
N
groups with different distributions. Each group
k
has an averaged loss
L_{k}(w)=\frac{1}{n_{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{n_{k}}\ell(f_{w}(x^{k}_{i}),y^{k}_{i})
, where
w
is the the model parameter and
(x^{k}_{i},y^{k}_{i})
is a data point. For robust optimization, we assign different weights to different groups and form the following robust loss minimization problem:

\min_{w}\max_{p\in\Omega}\sum_{k=1}^{N}p_{k}L_{k}(w),

where
\Omega\subset\Delta
and
\Delta
denotes a simplex. A common choice for
\Omega
is
\Omega=\{\mathbf{p}\in\Delta,p_{i}\leq 1/K\}
where
K
is an integer, resulting in the so-called CVaR losses, i.e., average of top-K group losses. Consequently, the above problem can be equivalently reformulated as
[
23
]
:

\min_{w}\min_{s}F(w,s)=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{N}[L_{k}(w)-s]_{+}+s.

This formulation can be mapped into non-smooth weakly-convex FCCO when the loss function
\ell(\cdot,\cdot)
is weakly convex in terms of
w
. In comparison to directly solving the min-max problem, solving the above FCCO problem avoids the need of dealing with the projection onto the constraint
\Omega
and expensive sampling as in existing works
[
4
]
.
